   248→              ;

   250→          modules = [
   251→            ./nix/modules/darwin
   252→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   253→            ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
   254→            {
   255→              nixpkgs.overlays = [

   263→          ]

   266→    in
   267→    flake-parts.lib.mkFlake { inherit inputs; } {
   268→      systems = [
   269→        "x86_64-linux"
   270→        "aarch64-darwin"
   271→      ];
   272→
   273→      imports = [
   274→        treefmt-nix.flakeModule
   275→        inputs.git-hooks.flakeModule
   276→      ];
   277→
   278→      perSystem =
   279→        {
   280→          pkgs,
   281→          system,
   282→          config,
   283→          ...
   284→        }:
   285→        let
   286→          isDarwin = builtins.match ".*-darwin" system != null;
   287→          hostname = if isDarwin then "darwin" else "nixos";
   288→          nom = "${pkgs.nix-output-monitor}/bin/nom";
   289→          customPkgs = import nixpkgs {
   290→            inherit system;
   291→            overlays = [ customOverlay ];
   292→          };
   293→        in
   294→        {
   295→          packages = {
   296→            inherit (customPkgs)
   297→              difit
   298→              entire
   299→              jj-desc
   300→              keifu
   301→              polycat
   302→              pretty-ts-errors-markdown
   303→              similarity-ts
   304→              ;
   305→          };
   306→          apps = {
   307→            build = {
   308→              type = "app";
   309→              program = toString (
   310→                pkgs.writeShellScript "build" ''
   311→                  set -e
   312→                  echo "Building ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   313→                  ${nom} build ${
   314→                    if isDarwin then
   315→                      ".#darwinConfigurations.${hostname}.system"
   316→                    else
   317→                      ".#nixosConfigurations.${hostname}.config.system.build.toplevel"
   318→                  }
   319→                  echo "Build successful! Run 'nix run .#switch' to apply."
   320→                ''
   321→              );
   322→            };
   323→            switch = {
   324→              type = "app";
   325→              program = toString (
   326→                pkgs.writeShellScript "switch" ''
   327→                  set -eo pipefail
   328→                  echo "Switching to ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   329→                  ${
   330→                    if isDarwin then
   331→                      "darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   332→                    else
   333→                      "sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   334→                  }
   335→                  echo "Done!"
   336→                ''
   337→              );
   338→            };
   339→            fmt = {
   340→              type = "app";
   341→              program = toString (
   342→                pkgs.writeShellScript "treefmt-wrapper" ''
   343→                  exec ${config.treefmt.build.wrapper}/bin/treefmt "$@"
   344→                ''
   345→              );
   346→            };
   347→          };
   348→
   349→          treefmt = {
   350→            projectRootFile = "flake.nix";
   351→            programs = {
   352→              nixfmt.enable = true;
   353→              stylua.enable = true;
   354→              shfmt.enable = true;
   355→            };
   356→            settings.global.excludes = [
   357→              ".git/**"
   358→              "*.lock"
   359→            ];
   360→          };
   361→
   362→          pre-commit = {
   363→            check.enable = false;
   364→            settings.hooks = {
   365→              treefmt = {
   366→                enable = true;
   367→                package = config.treefmt.build.wrapper;
   368→              };
   369→              git-secrets = {
   370→                enable = true;
   371→                entry = "${pkgs.git-secrets}/bin/git-secrets --pre_commit_hook";
   372→                language = "system";
   373→                stages = [ "pre-commit" ];
   374→                excludes = [ "^\.direnv/" ];
   375→              };
   376→            };
   377→          };
   378→
   379→          devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell {
   380→            shellHook = ''
   381→              ${config.pre-commit.installationScript}
   382→            '';
   383→          };
   384→        };
   385→
   386→      flake = {
   387→        nixosConfigurations = {
   388→          nixos = mkSystem {
   389→            host = "nixos";

   391→            profile = "gui";
   392→            extraModules = [ ];

{"result":"The minimal flake configuration for a Nix-on-Droid host involves defining inputs for `nixpkgs` and `nix-on-droid`, and then using the `nix-on-droid.lib.nixOnDroidConfiguration` function to set up the system with your desired modules and packages . To integrate `home-manager`, you include it as an input and specify its path within the `nixOnDroidConfiguration` call  .\n\n### Minimal Flake Configuration\n\nA minimal `flake.nix` for Nix-on-Droid, including `home-manager` and basic settings, would look like this:\n\n```nix\n{\n  description = \"Nix-on-Droid configuration with Home Manager\";\n\n  inputs = {\n    nixpkgs.url = \"github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-24.05\";\n    home-manager = {\n      url = \"github:nix-community/home-manager/release-24.05\";\n      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n    };\n    nix-on-droid = {\n      url = \"github:nix-community/nix-on-droid/release-24.05\";\n      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n      inputs.home-manager.follows = \"home-manager\";\n    };\n  };\n\n  outputs = { self, nixpkgs, home-manager, nix-on-droid }: {\n    nixOnDroidConfigurations.default = nix-on-droid.lib.nixOnDroidConfiguration {\n      modules = [\n        ./nix-on-droid.nix\n      ];\n      pkgs = import nixpkgs {\n        system = \"aarch64-linux\";\n        overlays = [\n          nix-on-droid.overlays.default\n        ];\n      };\n      home-manager-path = home-manager.outPath;\n    };\n  };\n}\n``` \n\nThis `flake.nix` defines the `nixpkgs`, `home-manager`, and `nix-on-droid` inputs, ensuring they follow the specified versions . The `outputs` section then defines a `default` Nix-on-Droid configuration using `nix-on-droid.lib.nixOnDroidConfiguration` . It imports your local `nix-on-droid.nix` file as a module , sets the `pkgs` instance for `aarch64-linux` with the `nix-on-droid` overlay , and specifies the `home-manager-path` .\n\n### Minimal `nix-on-droid.nix` Example\n\nAlongside the `flake.nix`, you would have a `nix-on-droid.nix` file that contains your system and user-specific configurations. A minimal example that includes `home-manager` and basic settings would be:\n\n```nix\n{ config, lib, pkgs, ... }:\n\n{\n  environment.packages = with pkgs; [\n    vim\n  ];\n\n  system.stateVersion = \"24.05\";\n\n  nix.extraOptions = ''\n    experimental-features = nix-command flakes\n  '';\n\n  home-manager = {\n    backupFileExtension = \"hm-bak\";\n    useGlobalPkgs = true;\n\n    config =\n      { config, lib, pkgs, ... }:\n      {\n        home.stateVersion = \"24.05\";\n        # insert home-manager config here, e.g., programs.git.enable = true;\n      };\n  };\n}\n``` \n\nThis `nix-on-droid.nix` file installs `vim` as an example package , sets the `system.stateVersion` to \"24.05\" , and enables Nix experimental features for flakes . It also configures `home-manager` to use global packages and sets its `home.stateVersion` .\n\nTo activate this configuration on your Nix-on-Droid device, you would use the command:\n`nix-on-droid switch --flake .#default` .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `system.stateVersion` and `home.stateVersion` values should correspond to the NixOS and Home Manager release versions you are using  . It is important to read the changelogs before updating these values . The `pkgs` argument in `nixOnDroidConfiguration` should specify the target system, typically `aarch64-linux` for most Android devices .\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Overview (nix-community/nix-on-droid)](/wiki/nix-community/nix-on-droid#1)\n- [Package Management (nix-community/nix-on-droid)](/wiki/nix-community/nix-on-droid#2.2)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

両ホストのファイルを作成しました。構成は以下の通りです:

**`nix/hosts/OPPO-A79/`** & **`nix/hosts/Galaxy-S23FE/`**
- `default.nix` — `nix-on-droid.lib.nixOnDroidConfiguration` を呼び出すエントリポイント
- `nix-on-droid.nix` — デバイス固有の設定（`git`, `openssh`、flakes有効化、home-manager基本設定）

デバイスごとにパッケージや設定をカスタマイズする場合は、各 `nix-on-droid.nix` を編集してください。

```
     1→# dotnix
     2→
     3→[![DeepWiki](https://img.shields.io/badge/DeepWiki-yutakobayashidev%2Fdotnix-blue.svg?logo=data:image/png;base64,REDACTED)](https://deepwiki.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix)
     4→
     5→NixOS & macOS (nix-darwin) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager.
     6→
     7→## Target
     8→
     9→| Machine | Name | OS | System | Stable |
    10→|---|---|---|---|---|
    11→| UM790 Pro | nixos | NixOS | x86_64-linux | ◎ |
    12→| M2 MacBook Air | darwin | macOS | aarch64-darwin | ◎ |
    13→
    14→
    15→## Module Structure
    16→
    17→```
    18→flake.nix                    # Entry point (nixosConfigurations + darwinConfigurations)
    19→├── nix/
    20→│   ├── hosts/
    21→│   │   ├── nixos/           # NixOS host config (boot, network, locale)
    22→│   │   └── darwin/          # macOS host config
    23→│   ├── profiles/
    24→│   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix  # Minimal CLI environment
    25→│   │   ├── cli.nix          # CLI environment (docker, tailscale)
    26→│   │   ├── cli-server.nix   # Server CLI environment (docker, no tailscale)
    27→│   │   ├── gui.nix          # GUI environment (niri, audio, bluetooth)
    28→│   │   ├── laptop.nix       # Laptop environment (gui + extras)
    29→│   │   └── darwin.nix       # macOS environment
    30→│   ├── modules/
    31→│   │   ├── linux/           # NixOS system modules (niri, docker, audio, etc.)
    32→│   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS nix-darwin modules (homebrew, system defaults)
    33→│   │   └── home/            # Home Manager shared modules (zsh, git, claude-code)
    34→│   └── overlays/            # Custom packages (overlay)
    35→├── agents/skills/           # Claude Code agent skills
    36→├── nvim/                    # Neovim config (Lua)
    37→└── zsh/                     # Zsh config
    38→```
    39→
    40→## Documentation
    41→
    42→- [docs/UM790Pro.md](docs/UM790Pro.md) - NixOS installation guide
    43→- [docs/M2-MacBook-Air.md](docs/M2-MacBook-Air.md) - nix-darwin installation guide for macOS
    44→
    45→## Daily Usage
    46→
    47→```sh
    48→# Apply changes (NixOS or macOS)
    49→nix run .#switch
    50→
    51→# Build without applying
    52→nix run .#build
    53→
    54→# Format all files (nix, lua, sh)
    55→nix run .#fmt
    56→
    57→# Update flake inputs
    58→nix flake update
    59→```
    60→
    61→## Available Nix Apps
    62→
    63→### NixOS
    64→
    65→- `nix run .#switch` - Build and apply NixOS + Home Manager configuration (`sudo nixos-rebuild switch`)
    66→- `nix run .#build` - Build configuration without applying
    67→- `nix run .#fmt` - Format all files (nix, lua, sh) via [treefmt](https://github.com/numtide/treefmt-nix)
    68→
    69→### macOS
    70→
    71→- `nix run .#switch` - Build and apply nix-darwin + Home Manager configuration (`darwin-rebuild switch`)
    72→- `nix run .#build` - Build configuration without applying
    73→- `nix run .#fmt` - Format all files (nix, lua, sh) via [treefmt](https://github.com/numtide/treefmt-nix)
    74→
    75→Both use [nix-output-monitor](https://github.com/maralorn/nix-output-monitor) for build output.
    76→
    77→## Key Features
    78→
    79→### NixOS
    80→
    81→- **WM**: [Niri](https://github.com/YaLTeR/niri) (scrollable tiling Wayland compositor)
    82→- **IME**: fcitx5 + [hazkey](https://github.com/aster-void/nix-hazkey) (LLM-powered Japanese input)
    83→- **YubiKey**: PAM U2F authentication (polkit, swaylock)
    84→- **Development**: Docker, Tailscale, Android development environment
    85→
    86→### macOS
    87→
    88→- **Homebrew**: GUI app management via casks (Ghostty, Chrome, OrbStack, etc.)
    89→- **brew-nix**: Homebrew cask packages managed as Nix packages (version pinning & rollback)
    90→- **Touch ID**: sudo authentication support
    91→- **1Password**: Shell Plugins (gh, awscli2)
    92→
    93→## Managed Tools
    94→
    95→- **AI Development**: claude-code, codex, opencode, ccusage, vibe-kanban
    96→- **Version Control**: git, lazygit, jujutsu (jj), git-lfs, git-wt
    97→- **Core CLI**: ripgrep, fzf, jq, zoxide, lsd, btop, yazi, tmux
    98→- **Communication**: halloy (IRC)
    99→- **Editors**: Neovim, VSCode
   100→- **Terminal**: Ghostty, Zsh + Oh My Zsh
   101→- **Development**: Node.js, Bun, MoonBit, Google Cloud SDK, Typst
   102→- **Network**: nmap, bandwhich, speedtest-cli
   103→
   104→## Agent Skills
   105→
   106→Claude Code skills are managed via [agent-skills-nix](https://github.com/Kyure-A/agent-skills-nix).
   107→
   108→- **Config**: `nix/modules/home/agent-skills.nix`
   109→- **Local skills**: `agents/skills/`
   110→- **External skills**: [anthropics/skills](https://github.com/anthropics/skills), [vercel-labs/skills](https://github.com/vercel-labs/skills), [ast-grep/claude-skill](https://github.com/ast-grep/claude-skill)
   111→- **Deploy targets**: `~/.agents/skills`, `~/.config/claude/skills`, `~/.config/codex/skills`
   112→
   113→Key skills: `social-digest`, `oura-daily-watch`, `gha-lint`, `check-similarity`, `dce` (local), `docx`, `pdf`, `pptx`, `xlsx`, `frontend-design`, `webapp-testing`, `skill-creator` (Anthropic), `find-skills` (Vercel), `ast-grep` (ast-grep), `ui-ux-pro-max` (community)
   114→
   115→
```

  "old_string": "NixOS & macOS (nix-darwin) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager.\n\n## Target\n\n| Machine | Name | OS | System | Stable |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| UM790 Pro | nixos | NixOS | x86_64-linux | ◎ |\n| M2 MacBook Air | darwin | macOS | aarch64-darwin | ◎ |\n\n\n## Module Structure\n\n```\nflake.nix                    # Entry point (nixosConfigurations + darwinConfigurations)\n├── nix/\n│   ├── hosts/\n│   │   ├── nixos/           # NixOS host config (boot, network, locale)\n│   │   └── darwin/          # macOS host config\n│   ├── profiles/\n│   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix  # Minimal CLI environment\n│   │   ├── cli.nix          # CLI environment (docker, tailscale)\n│   │   ├── cli-server.nix   # Server CLI environment (docker, no tailscale)\n│   │   ├── gui.nix          # GUI environment (niri, audio, bluetooth)\n│   │   ├── laptop.nix       # Laptop environment (gui + extras)\n│   │   └── darwin.nix       # macOS environment\n│   ├── modules/\n│   │   ├── linux/           # NixOS system modules (niri, docker, audio, etc.)\n│   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS nix-darwin modules (homebrew, system defaults)\n│   │   └── home/            # Home Manager shared modules (zsh, git, claude-code)\n│   └── overlays/            # Custom packages (overlay)\n├── agents/skills/           # Claude Code agent skills\n├── nvim/                    # Neovim config (Lua)\n└── zsh/                     # Zsh config\n```",
  "new_string": "NixOS, macOS (nix-darwin) & Android (nix-on-droid) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager (nixos-unstable).\n\n## Target\n\n| Machine | Name | OS | System | Stable |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| UM790 Pro | nixos | NixOS | x86_64-linux | ◎ |\n| M2 MacBook Air | darwin | macOS | aarch64-darwin | ◎ |\n| Galaxy S23 FE | Galaxy-S23FE | Android (nix-on-droid) | aarch64-linux | △ |\n\n\n## Module Structure\n\n```\nflake.nix                    # Entry point (nixos + darwin + nixOnDroid Configurations)\n├── nix/\n│   ├── hosts/\n│   │   ├── nixos/           # NixOS host config (boot, network, locale)\n│   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS host config\n│   │   └── Galaxy-S23FE/    # nix-on-droid host config\n│   ├── profiles/\n│   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix  # Minimal CLI environment\n│   │   ├── cli.nix          # CLI environment (docker, tailscale)\n│   │   ├── cli-server.nix   # Server CLI environment (docker, no tailscale)\n│   │   ├── gui.nix          # GUI environment (niri, audio, bluetooth)\n│   │   ├── laptop.nix       # Laptop environment (gui + extras)\n│   │   └── darwin.nix       # macOS environment\n│   ├── modules/\n│   │   ├── linux/           # NixOS system modules (niri, docker, audio, etc.)\n│   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS nix-darwin modules (homebrew, system defaults)\n│   │   ├── nix-on-droid/    # nix-on-droid shared modules\n│   │   └── home/            # Home Manager shared modules (zsh, git, claude-code)\n│   └── overlays/            # Custom packages (overlay)\n├── agents/skills/           # Claude Code agent skills\n├── nvim/                    # Neovim config (Lua)\n└── zsh/                     # Zsh config\n```"

# Yuta's NixOS & macOS Configuration - Flake File

This is a comprehensive Nix flake configuration for managing both NixOS and macOS systems. Here are the key components:

## Main Inputs
The configuration pulls from numerous flake repositories including:
- **Core**: nixpkgs, home-manager, nix-darwin
- **Tools**: ghostty, niri, treefmt-nix
- **Custom tools**: gh-nippou, gh-graph, version-lsp
- **Skills/Agents**: anthropic-skills, vercel-skills, ast-grep-skill

## Key Features

**Helper Functions**: Defines `mkSystem` for NixOS and `mkDarwin` for macOS configurations, each with specialized module loading and overlays.

**Package Management**: Implements custom overlays including external packages from llm-agents and nix-steipete-tools, conditional on system architecture.

**Build Commands**: Provides `nix run` apps for building and switching configurations, with conditional logic for Darwin vs. NixOS systems.

**Code Formatting**: Integrates treefmt with nixfmt, stylua, and shfmt, plus pre-commit hooks for git-secrets validation.

**Supported Systems**: 
- x86_64-linux (NixOS)
- aarch64-darwin (Apple Silicon macOS)

The flake uses both NixOS and nix-darwin modules to maintain declarative configuration across Linux and macOS platforms.
```

   102→      "yuta.cachix.org-1:REDACTED"

   105→
   106→  outputs =
   107→    inputs@{
   108→      self,
   109→      nixpkgs,
   110→      flake-parts,
   111→      treefmt-nix,
   112→      ghostty,
   113→      home-manager,
   114→      niri,
   115→      llm-agents,
   116→      nix-steipete-tools,
   117→      gh-nippou,
   118→      gh-graph,
   119→      nix-hazkey,
   120→      version-lsp,
   121→      agent-skills,
   122→      anthropic-skills,
   123→      vercel-skills,
   124→      ui-ux-pro-max-skill,
   125→      ast-grep-skill,
   126→      nix-darwin,
   127→      onepassword-shell-plugins,
   128→      brew-nix,
   129→      moonbit-overlay,
   130→      nix-filter,
   131→      ...
   132→    }:
   133→    let
   134→      # Helper functions for home-manager
   135→      helpers = import ./nix/modules/lib/helpers { lib = nixpkgs.lib; };
   136→
   137→      # Dotfiles directory path helper
   138→      mkDotfilesDir = homeDir: "${homeDir}/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix";
   139→
   140→      # Local agent skills (filtered from this repo)
   141→      local-skills = nix-filter.lib {
   142→        root = self;
   143→        include = [ "agents/skills" ];
   144→      };
   145→
   146→      # Overlay for external flake packages (parameterized by system)
   147→      mkExternalOverlay =
   148→        system: final: prev:
   149→        {
   150→          claude-code = llm-agents.packages.${system}.claude-code;
   151→          ccusage = llm-agents.packages.${system}.ccusage;
   152→          codex = llm-agents.packages.${system}.codex;
   153→          opencode = llm-agents.packages.${system}.opencode;
   154→          vibe-kanban = llm-agents.packages.${system}.vibe-kanban;
   155→          cursor-agent = llm-agents.packages.${system}.cursor-agent;
   156→          gogcli = nix-steipete-tools.packages.${system}.gogcli;
   157→          version-lsp = version-lsp.packages.${system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
   158→            doCheck = false;
   159→          });
   160→          gh-nippou = gh-nippou.packages.${system}.default;
   161→          gh-graph = gh-graph.packages.${system}.default;
   162→        }
   163→        // (
   164→          if builtins.match ".*-linux" system != null then
   165→            {
   166→              ghostty = ghostty.packages.${system}.default;
   167→            }
   168→          else
   169→            { }
   170→        );
   171→
   172→      # Custom package overlays
   173→      customOverlay = import ./nix/overlays;
   174→
   175→      # mkSystem helper function (NixOS)
   176→      mkSystem =
   177→        {
   178→          host,
   179→          system,
   180→          profile,
   181→          extraModules ? [ ],
   182→        }:
   183→        let
   184→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/home/yuta";
   185→        in
   186→        nixpkgs.lib.nixosSystem {
   187→          inherit system;
   188→          specialArgs = {
   189→            inherit
   190→              helpers
   191→              dotfilesDir
   192→              home-manager
   193→              niri
   194→              local-skills
   195→              anthropic-skills
   196→              vercel-skills
   197→              ui-ux-pro-max-skill
   198→              ast-grep-skill
   199→              agent-skills
   200→              ;
   201→          };
   202→          modules = [
   203→            ./nix/modules/linux
   204→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   205→            ./nix/profiles/${profile}.nix
   206→            {
   207→              nixpkgs.overlays = [
   208→                (mkExternalOverlay system)
   209→                moonbit-overlay.overlays.default
   210→                customOverlay
   211→              ];
   212→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfreePredicate =
   213→                pkg:
   214→                builtins.elem (nixpkgs.lib.getName pkg) [
   215→                  "claude-code"
   216→                  "android-studio"
   217→                  "google-chrome"
   218→                  "discord"
   219→                  "slack"
   220→                  "obsidian"
   221→                  "1password"
   222→                  "insomnia"
   223→                  "spotify"
   224→                ];
   225→            }
   226→            nix-hazkey.nixosModules.hazkey
   227→          ]
   228→          ++ extraModules;
   229→        };
   230→
   231→      # mkDarwin helper function (macOS)
   232→      mkDarwin =
   233→        {
   234→          host,
   235→          system,
   236→          extraModules ? [ ],
   237→        }:
   238→        let
   239→          dotfilesDir = mkDotfilesDir "/Users/yuta";
   240→        in
   241→        nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
   242→          inherit system;
   243→          specialArgs = {
   244→            inherit
   245→              helpers
   246→              dotfilesDir
   247→              home-manager
   248→              local-skills
   249→              anthropic-skills
   250→              vercel-skills
   251→              ui-ux-pro-max-skill
   252→              ast-grep-skill
   253→              agent-skills
   254→              onepassword-shell-plugins
   255→              ;
   256→          };
   257→          modules = [
   258→            ./nix/modules/darwin
   259→            ./nix/hosts/${host}
   260→            ./nix/profiles/darwin.nix
   261→            {
   262→              nixpkgs.overlays = [
   263→                (mkExternalOverlay system)
   264→                moonbit-overlay.overlays.default
   265→                customOverlay
   266→                brew-nix.overlays.default
   267→              ];
   268→              nixpkgs.config.allowUnfree = true;
   269→            }
   270→          ]
   271→          ++ extraModules;
   272→        };
   273→    in
   274→    flake-parts.lib.mkFlake { inherit inputs; } {
   275→      systems = [
   276→        "x86_64-linux"
   277→        "aarch64-darwin"
   278→      ];
   279→
   280→      imports = [
   281→        treefmt-nix.flakeModule
   282→        inputs.git-hooks.flakeModule
   283→      ];
   284→
   285→      perSystem =
   286→        {
   287→          pkgs,
   288→          system,
   289→          config,
   290→          ...
   291→        }:
   292→        let
   293→          isDarwin = builtins.match ".*-darwin" system != null;
   294→          hostname = if isDarwin then "darwin" else "nixos";
   295→          nom = "${pkgs.nix-output-monitor}/bin/nom";
   296→          customPkgs = import nixpkgs {
   297→            inherit system;
   298→            overlays = [ customOverlay ];
   299→          };
   300→        in
   301→        {
   302→          packages = {
   303→            inherit (customPkgs)
   304→              difit
   305→              entire
   306→              jj-desc
   307→              keifu
   308→              polycat
   309→              pretty-ts-errors-markdown
   310→              similarity-ts
   311→              ;
   312→          };
   313→          apps = {
   314→            build = {
   315→              type = "app";
   316→              program = toString (
   317→                pkgs.writeShellScript "build" ''
   318→                  set -e
   319→                  echo "Building ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   320→                  ${nom} build ${
   321→                    if isDarwin then
   322→                      ".#darwinConfigurations.${hostname}.system"
   323→                    else
   324→                      ".#nixosConfigurations.${hostname}.config.system.build.toplevel"
   325→                  }
   326→                  echo "Build successful! Run 'nix run .#switch' to apply."
   327→                ''
   328→              );
   329→            };
   330→            switch = {
   331→              type = "app";
   332→              program = toString (
   333→                pkgs.writeShellScript "switch" ''
   334→                  set -eo pipefail
   335→                  echo "Switching to ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   336→                  ${
   337→                    if isDarwin then
   338→                      "darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   339→                    else
   340→                      "sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   341→                  }
   342→                  echo "Done!"
   343→                ''
   344→              );
   345→            };
   346→            fmt = {
   347→              type = "app";
   348→              program = toString (
   349→                pkgs.writeShellScript "treefmt-wrapper" ''
   350→                  exec ${config.treefmt.build.wrapper}/bin/treefmt "$@"
   351→                ''
   352→              );
   353→            };
   354→          };
   355→
   356→          treefmt = {
   357→            projectRootFile = "flake.nix";
   358→            programs = {
   359→              nixfmt.enable = true;
   360→              stylua.enable = true;
   361→              shfmt.enable = true;
   362→            };
   363→            settings.global.excludes = [
   364→              ".git/**"
   365→              "*.lock"
   366→            ];
   367→          };
   368→
   369→          pre-commit = {
   370→            check.enable = false;
   371→            settings.hooks = {
   372→              treefmt = {
   373→                enable = true;
   374→                package = config.treefmt.build.wrapper;
   375→              };
   376→              git-secrets = {
   377→                enable = true;
   378→                entry = "${pkgs.git-secrets}/bin/git-secrets --pre_commit_hook";
   379→                language = "system";
   380→                stages = [ "pre-commit" ];
   381→                excludes = [ "^\.direnv/" ];
   382→              };
   383→            };
   384→          };
   385→
   386→          devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell {
   387→            shellHook = ''
   388→              ${config.pre-commit.installationScript}
   389→            '';
   390→          };
   391→        };
   392→
   393→      flake = {
   394→        nixosConfigurations = {
   395→          nixos = mkSystem {
   396→            host = "nixos";
   397→            system = "x86_64-linux";
   398→            profile = "gui";
   399→            extraModules = [ ];
   400→          };
   401→        };
   402→
   403→        darwinConfigurations = {
   404→          darwin = mkDarwin {
   405→            host = "darwin";
   406→            system = "aarch64-darwin";
   407→          };
   408→        };
   409→
   410→        nixOnDroidConfigurations = {
   411→          Galaxy-S23FE = import ./nix/hosts/Galaxy-S23FE { inherit inputs; };
   412→        };
   413→      };
   414→    };
   415→}
   416→

# NixOS System Configuration File

This is a NixOS flake configuration that sets up a complete system for user "take" on x86_64-linux architecture.

## Key Components

**External Dependencies:**
The configuration imports several flake inputs including nixpkgs, home-manager, disko (disk configuration), impermanence (ephemeral root filesystem), and hardware-specific modules for a Lenovo ThinkPad X13 AMD.

**System Setup:**
- Username: "take"
- Platform: x86_64-linux
- Hardware: Lenovo ThinkPad X13 AMD

**Storage Configuration:**
"boot.initrd.luks.devices."cryptroot" = { device = "/dev/disk/by-partlabel/cryptroot"; allowDiscards = true; bypassWorkqueues = true; }"

This enables LUKS encryption with systemd support in the initrd for both encrypted storage and impermanent filesystem functionality.

**Home Manager Integration:**
The file configures home-manager with numerous specialized modules including nixvim (Neovim configuration), zen-browser, and various agent skills packages (Anthropic, Cloudflare, HashiCorp, AWS, Microsoft, Deno).

**Additional Modules:**
Multiple development and tool overlays are imported, such as emacs-overlay, firefox-addons, and brew-nix for macOS package compatibility.
```

# Nix Darwin Configuration File

This is a complete Nix configuration file for setting up a macOS system using nix-darwin and home-manager.

## Key Components

**System Setup:**
The configuration targets `aarch64-darwin` (Apple Silicon) with username "take" and imports several flake inputs including nix-darwin, home-manager, nixvim, and various overlays.

**Package Management:**
It establishes a package set by importing nixpkgs with custom overlays for advanced functionality and brew integration. The file also pulls in packages from NUR (Nix User Repository).

**Emacs Integration:**
"The configuration includes a dedicated emacs package import and creates a shared emacs library for use across nix-darwin and home-manager" modules, enabling Emacs utilities in the aerospace window manager.

**Module Structure:**
The nix-darwin system composition includes modules for virtualization (darwin-vz-nix), package management (brew-nix), macOS app utilities, and comprehensive home-manager setup with nixvim support.

**Home Manager Configuration:**
Home-manager is configured to manage user packages and dotfiles, with special arguments passed to shared modules including browser configurations, development tools, and various agent skill modules for AI/LLM integration.

The setup reflects a sophisticated development environment leveraging declarative system management on macOS.
```

# Nix Flake Configuration Overview

This is a comprehensive Nix flake configuration file for managing system setups across multiple platforms. Here's what it contains:

## Key Sections

**Description & Purpose:**
The file represents "takeokunn's nix configuration," serving as a declarative specification for system management.

**Input Sources:**
The configuration pulls from numerous Nix ecosystem repositories, including:
- Core packages from nixpkgs (both unstable and stable versions)
- Development tools (emacs-overlay, nixvim, home-manager)
- System management (nix-darwin for macOS, nixos-hardware, nix-on-droid)
- Utility flakes (disko, impermanence, treefmt-nix)
- Browser configurations (zen-browser, firefox-addons)
- Agent skills from various providers (Anthropic, Cloudflare, HashiCorp, Deno, AWS, Microsoft)
- Custom packages repository (nur-packages)

**Target Systems:**
The configuration defines three system setups:
1. **Attm-M4-Max** - A macOS installation (darwin)
2. **X13Gen2** - A NixOS Linux system
3. **OPPO-A79** - An Android device running Nix on Droid

**Development Tools:**
The flake includes formatters for multiple languages: Nix, YAML, TOML, Fish shell, Lua, and shell scripts, plus GitHub Actions linting.

This represents a sophisticated, multi-platform infrastructure-as-code approach using Nix's declarative configuration system.
```

{
  description = "takeokunn's nix configuration";

  inputs = {
    nixpkgs.url = "github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixpkgs-unstable";
    nixpkgs-stable.url = "github:nixos/nixpkgs/nixos-25.11";
    emacs-overlay = {
      url = "github:nix-community/emacs-overlay";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
      inputs.nixpkgs-stable.follows = "nixpkgs-stable";
    };
    org-babel.url = "github:emacs-twist/org-babel";
    home-manager = {
      url = "github:nix-community/home-manager";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    nixos-hardware.url = "github:NixOS/nixos-hardware";
    xremap = {
      url = "github:xremap/nix-flake";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    nix-darwin = {
      url = "github:LnL7/nix-darwin";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    # TODO: Pinned to specific nixpkgs commit as workaround for nix-community/nix-on-droid#495
    # Issue: "getting pseudoterminal attributes: Permission denied" with nixpkgs after 2026-01-24
    nix-on-droid = {
      url = "github:nix-community/nix-on-droid";
      inputs.nixpkgs.url = "github:NixOS/nixpkgs/2bceeb45e516fc6956714014c92ddfdafe4c9da3";
      inputs.home-manager.follows = "home-manager";
    };
    flake-parts.url = "github:hercules-ci/flake-parts";
    treefmt-nix = {
      url = "github:numtide/treefmt-nix";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    mcp-servers-nix = {
      url = "github:natsukium/mcp-servers-nix";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    disko = {
      url = "github:nix-community/disko";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    impermanence = {
      url = "github:nix-community/impermanence";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
      inputs.home-manager.follows = "home-manager";
    };
    nixvim = {
      url = "github:nix-community/nixvim";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    brew-nix = {
      url = "github:BatteredBunny/brew-nix";
      inputs.brew-api.follows = "brew-api";
    };
    brew-api = {
      url = "github:BatteredBunny/brew-api";
      flake = false;
    };
    mac-app-util.url = "github:hraban/mac-app-util";
    llm-agents = {
      url = "github:numtide/llm-agents.nix";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
      inputs.treefmt-nix.follows = "treefmt-nix";
    };
    agent-skills = {
      url = "github:Kyure-A/agent-skills-nix";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
      inputs.home-manager.follows = "home-manager";
    };
    anthropic-skills = {
      url = "github:anthropics/skills";
      flake = false;
    };
    cloudflare-skills = {
      url = "github:cloudflare/skills";
      flake = false;
    };
    hashicorp-agent-skills = {

      flake = false;

    deno-skills = {

      flake = false;

      url = "github:microsoft/skills";
      flake = false;

      url = "github:0xc000022070/zen-browser-flake";
      inputs = {
        nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
        home-manager.follows = "home-manager";
      };
    };
    firefox-addons = {
      url = "gitlab:rycee/nur-expressions?dir=pkgs/firefox-addons";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    nur-packages = {
      url = "github:takeokunn/nur-packages";
      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
    };
    darwin-vz-nix.url = "github:takeokunn/darwin-vz-nix";
  };

  outputs =
    {
      flake-parts,
      treefmt-nix,
      ...
    }@inputs:

    flake-parts.lib.mkFlake { inherit inputs; } {
      systems = [
        "aarch64-darwin"
        "aarch64-linux"
        "x86_64-linux"
      ];

      imports = [ treefmt-nix.flakeModule ];

      flake = {
        darwinConfigurations = {
          Attm-M4-Max = import ./hosts/Attm-M4-Max { inherit inputs; };
        };
        nixosConfigurations = {
          X13Gen2 = import ./hosts/X13Gen2 { inherit inputs; };
        };
        nixOnDroidConfigurations = {
          OPPO-A79 = import ./hosts/OPPO-A79 { inherit inputs; };
        };
      };

      perSystem =
        { ... }:
        {
          treefmt = {
            projectRootFile = "flake.nix";
            programs = {
              actionlint.enable = true;
              nixfmt.enable = true;
              taplo.enable = true;
              yamlfmt.enable = true;
              fish_indent.enable = true;
              stylua.enable = true;
              shfmt.enable = true;
            };
          };
        };
    };
}

```
{ inputs }:
let
  inherit (inputs)
    nix-darwin
    home-manager
    nixvim
    brew-nix
    mac-app-util
    emacs-overlay
    nur-packages
    darwin-vz-nix
    ;
  inherit (inputs) nixpkgs;

  username = "take";
  system = "aarch64-darwin";

  advancedOverlay = import ../../home-manager/overlay/advanced.nix { inherit emacs-overlay; };

  pkgs = import nixpkgs {
    inherit system;
    config.allowUnfree = true;
    overlays = advancedOverlay ++ [ brew-nix.overlays.default ];
  };

  nurPkgs = nur-packages.packages.${system};

  # emacs package (for nix-darwin to use in aerospace)
  emacs = import ../../home-manager/packages/emacs {
    inherit (nixpkgs) lib;
    inherit pkgs nurPkgs;
  };
  emacsPkg = emacs.emacs-unstable;

  # emacs library (shared utilities for both nix-darwin and home-manager)
  emacsLib = import ../../home-manager/lib/emacs.nix {
    inherit (nixpkgs) lib;
    inherit pkgs emacsPkg;
  };

  configuration =
    { ... }:
    {
      users.users.${username}.home = "/Users/${username}";
    };
in
nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
  inherit pkgs;
  inherit (inputs.nixpkgs) lib;
  specialArgs = {
    inherit
      inputs
      username
      pkgs
      emacsLib
      ;
  };
  modules = [
    configuration
    ../../nix-darwin
    darwin-vz-nix.darwinModules.default
    brew-nix.darwinModules.default
    mac-app-util.darwinModules.default
    home-manager.darwinModules.home-manager
    {
      home-manager = {
        useUserPackages = true;
        users."${username}" = import ../../home-manager/advanced.nix;
        sharedModules = [
          nixvim.homeModules.nixvim
          mac-app-util.homeManagerModules.default
          inputs.zen-browser.homeModules.twilight
          inputs.agent-skills.homeManagerModules.default
        ];
        extraSpecialArgs = {
          inherit inputs system username;
          inherit (inputs) nixpkgs nixvim;
          inherit (inputs) mcp-servers-nix;
          inherit (inputs) llm-agents;
          inherit (inputs) nur-packages;
          inherit (inputs)
            anthropic-skills
            cloudflare-skills
            hashicorp-agent-skills
            deno-skills
            aws-agent-skills
            microsoft-skills
            ;
          inherit (inputs)
            emacs-overlay
            org-babel
            brew-nix
            firefox-addons
            ;
        };
      };
    }
  ];
}

```
{ inputs }:
let
  inherit (inputs) nixpkgs xremap nixos-hardware;
  inherit (inputs)
    home-manager
    disko
    nixvim
    impermanence
    ;

  username = "take";
  system = "x86_64-linux";
in
nixpkgs.lib.nixosSystem {
  inherit system;
  specialArgs = {
    inherit username xremap;
  };

  modules = [
    # External modules
    nixos-hardware.nixosModules.lenovo-thinkpad-x13-amd
    disko.nixosModules.disko
    impermanence.nixosModules.impermanence
    home-manager.nixosModules.home-manager

    # Local configuration
    ../../nixos
    ./hardware-configuration.nix
    ./disko-config.nix
    ./impermanence.nix
    {
      # LUKS configuration
      boot.initrd.luks.devices."cryptroot" = {
        device = "/dev/disk/by-partlabel/cryptroot";
        allowDiscards = true;
        bypassWorkqueues = true;
      };

      # Enable systemd in initrd for LUKS and impermanence
      boot.initrd.systemd.enable = true;
    }
    {
      home-manager.useUserPackages = true;
      home-manager.users."${username}" = import ../../home-manager/advanced.nix;
      home-manager.sharedModules = [
        nixvim.homeModules.nixvim
        inputs.zen-browser.homeModules.twilight
        inputs.agent-skills.homeManagerModules.default
      ];
      home-manager.extraSpecialArgs = {
        inherit inputs system;
        inherit (inputs)
          nixpkgs
          nixvim
          mcp-servers-nix
          llm-agents
          anthropic-skills
          cloudflare-skills
          hashicorp-agent-skills
          deno-skills
          aws-agent-skills
          microsoft-skills
          nur-packages
          emacs-overlay
          org-babel
          brew-nix
          firefox-addons
          ;
      };
    }
  ];
}
```

   273→    in

   277→        "aarch64-darwin"

   279→
   280→      imports = [

   285→      perSystem =
   286→        {
   287→          pkgs,
   288→          system,
   289→          config,

   291→        }:

   293→          isDarwin = builtins.match ".*-darwin" system != null;

   295→          nom = "${pkgs.nix-output-monitor}/bin/nom";
   296→          customPkgs = import nixpkgs {
   297→            inherit system;
   298→            overlays = [ customOverlay ];
   299→          };
   300→        in
   301→        {
   302→          packages = {
   303→            inherit (customPkgs)
   304→              difit

   311→              ;
   312→          };
   313→          apps = {
   314→            build = {
   315→              type = "app";
   316→              program = toString (
   317→                pkgs.writeShellScript "build" ''
   318→                  set -e
   319→                  echo "Building ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   320→                  ${nom} build ${
   321→                    if isDarwin then
   322→                      ".#darwinConfigurations.${hostname}.system"
   323→                    else
   324→                      ".#nixosConfigurations.${hostname}.config.system.build.toplevel"
   325→                  }
   326→                  echo "Build successful! Run 'nix run .#switch' to apply."
   327→                ''
   328→              );
   329→            };
   330→            switch = {
   331→              type = "app";
   332→              program = toString (
   333→                pkgs.writeShellScript "switch" ''
   334→                  set -eo pipefail
   335→                  echo "Switching to ${if isDarwin then "darwin" else "NixOS"} configuration..."
   336→                  ${
   337→                    if isDarwin then
   338→                      "darwin-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   339→                    else
   340→                      "sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#${hostname} |& ${nom}"
   341→                  }
   342→                  echo "Done!"
   343→                ''
   344→              );
   345→            };
   346→            fmt = {
   347→              type = "app";
   348→              program = toString (
   349→                pkgs.writeShellScript "treefmt-wrapper" ''
   350→                  exec ${config.treefmt.build.wrapper}/bin/treefmt "$@"
   351→                ''
   352→              );
   353→            };
   354→          };
   355→
   356→          treefmt = {
   357→            projectRootFile = "flake.nix";
   358→            programs = {
   359→              nixfmt.enable = true;
   360→              stylua.enable = true;
   361→              shfmt.enable = true;
   362→            };
   363→            settings.global.excludes = [
   364→              ".git/**"
   365→              "*.lock"
   366→            ];
   367→          };
   368→
   369→          pre-commit = {
   370→            check.enable = false;
   371→            settings.hooks = {
   372→              treefmt = {
   373→                enable = true;
   374→                package = config.treefmt.build.wrapper;
   375→              };
   376→              git-secrets = {
   377→                enable = true;
   378→                entry = "${pkgs.git-secrets}/bin/git-secrets --pre_commit_hook";
   379→                language = "system";
   380→                stages = [ "pre-commit" ];
   381→                excludes = [ "^\.direnv/" ];
   382→              };
   383→            };
   384→          };
   385→
   386→          devShells.default = pkgs.mkShell {
   387→            shellHook = ''
   388→              ${config.pre-commit.installationScript}
   389→            '';
   390→          };
   391→        };
   392→
   393→      flake = {
   394→        nixosConfigurations = {
   395→          nixos = mkSystem {
   396→            host = "nixos";
   397→            system = "x86_64-linux";
   398→            profile = "gui";
   399→            extraModules = [ ];
   400→          };
   401→        };
   402→
   403→        darwinConfigurations = {
   404→          darwin = mkDarwin {

   412→        };
   413→      };
   414→    };

> AGENT

リファクタリング完了です。変更の要約:

**`flake.nix`**: `mkSystem`/`mkDarwin` ヘルパー、`mkExternalOverlay`、`mkDotfilesDir`、`helpers`、`local-skills` をすべて削除。`outputs` の引数も `flake-parts` と `treefmt-nix` のみに簡素化。各ホストは `import ./nix/hosts/X { inherit inputs; }` で呼び出し。

**`nix/hosts/nixos/default.nix`** (新規): `{ inputs }` を受け取り、`nixpkgs.lib.nixosSystem` を直接呼び出す。overlays、specialArgs、modules、allowUnfree設定をすべて自己完結。

**`nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix`** (新規): 同様に `{ inputs }` を受け取り、`nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem` を直接呼び出す。

**`nix/hosts/Galaxy-S23FE/default.nix`**: 元々この形式だったので変更なし。

# OPPO-A79 Nix Installation Guide

This document provides setup instructions for installing a Nix-based environment on an OPPO-A79 Android device using nix-on-droid.

## Overview

The guide covers five core installation steps, beginning with app installation and culminating in applying a Nix configuration via flakes.

## Key Installation Steps

1. **App Installation**: Download nix-on-droid from F-Droid or GitHub releases
2. **Initial Setup**: Launch the app and allow the Nix installation process to finish
3. **Flakes Configuration**: Enable experimental features by creating a nix configuration file with the command: `"experimental-features = nix-command flakes"`
4. **Repository Setup**: Clone the nixos-configuration repository using git
5. **Apply Configuration**: Execute the switch command with flake parameters

## Maintenance

The same command used during installation (`"nix-on-droid switch --flake .#OPPO-A79 --show-trace"`) applies updates after the initial setup.

## System Details

- **Architecture**: aarch64-linux
- **Includes**: home-manager integration and nur-packages support
- **Requirements**: Network connectivity (WiFi preferred) and F-Droid or direct APK installation capability

#+STARTUP: fold
* First Installation (OPPO-A79)

OPPO-A79 is an Android device running nix-on-droid for a Nix-based environment on mobile.

** Prerequisites

- Android device (OPPO-A79 or compatible)
- F-Droid or direct APK installation capability
- Network connectivity (WiFi recommended)

** Step 1: Install nix-on-droid App

Download and install nix-on-droid from F-Droid or GitHub releases:

- F-Droid: https://f-droid.org/packages/com.termux.nix/
- GitHub: https://github.com/nix-community/nix-on-droid/releases

** Step 2: Initial Setup

Open the nix-on-droid app and wait for the initial Nix installation to complete.

** Step 3: Enable Flakes

Edit the nix-on-droid configuration to enable flakes:

#+begin_src shell
  mkdir -p ~/.config/nix
  echo "experimental-features = nix-command flakes" > ~/.config/nix/nix.conf
#+end_src

** Step 4: Clone Configuration

#+begin_src shell
  nix-shell -p git
  git clone https://github.com/takeokunn/nixos-configuration
  cd nixos-configuration
#+end_src

** Step 5: Apply Configuration

#+begin_src shell
  nix-on-droid switch --flake .#OPPO-A79 --show-trace
#+end_src

** Update Configuration

After installation, use this command to apply configuration changes:

#+begin_src shell
  nix-on-droid switch --flake .#OPPO-A79 --show-trace
#+end_src

** Notes

- This configuration includes home-manager with basic profile
- The system architecture is aarch64-linux
- nur-packages is available for additional packages

     1→# UM790 Pro (NixOS) Installation Guide
     2→
     3→## Initial Setup
     4→
     5→1. Install NixOS with the unstable channel
     6→
     7→2. Clone this repository:
     8→
     9→   ```sh
    10→   git clone https://github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix.git ~/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix
    11→   cd ~/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix
    12→   ```
    13→
    14→3. Apply the NixOS configuration:
    15→
    16→   ```sh
    17→   sudo nixos-rebuild switch --flake .#nixos
    18→   ```
    19→
    20→## YubiKey Setup
    21→
    22→YubiKeyでpolkit認証（1Passwordのロック解除など）を行うための設定。
    23→
    24→### 1. YubiKeyの登録
    25→
    26→```bash
    27→mkdir -p ~/.config/Yubico
    28→pamu2fcfg -o pam://nixos -i pam://nixos > ~/.config/Yubico/u2f_keys
    29→```
    30→
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/docs/Galaxy-S23FE.md",
  "content": "# Galaxy S23 FE (nix-on-droid) Installation Guide\n\nGalaxy S23 FE is an Android device running nix-on-droid for a Nix-based environment on mobile.\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Galaxy S23 FE (or compatible Android device)\n- F-Droid or direct APK installation capability\n- Network connectivity (WiFi recommended)\n\n## Step 1: Install nix-on-droid App\n\nDownload and install nix-on-droid from F-Droid or GitHub releases:\n\n- F-Droid: https://f-droid.org/packages/com.termux.nix/\n- GitHub: https://github.com/nix-community/nix-on-droid/releases\n\n## Step 2: Initial Setup\n\nOpen the nix-on-droid app and wait for the initial Nix installation to complete.\n\n## Step 3: Enable Flakes\n\n```sh\nmkdir -p ~/.config/nix\necho \"experimental-features = nix-command flakes\" > ~/.config/nix/nix.conf\n```\n\n## Step 4: Clone Configuration\n\n```sh\nnix-shell -p git\ngit clone https://github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix.git\ncd dotnix\n```\n\n## Step 5: Apply Configuration\n\n```sh\nnix-on-droid switch --flake .#Galaxy-S23FE --show-trace\n```\n\n## Update Configuration\n\nAfter installation, use this command to apply configuration changes:\n\n```sh\nnix-on-droid switch --flake .#Galaxy-S23FE --show-trace\n```\n"
}
```

-NixOS & macOS (nix-darwin) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager.
+NixOS, macOS (nix-darwin) & Android (nix-on-droid) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager (nixos-unstable).
 
 ## Target
 
@@ -10,16 +10,18 @@ NixOS & macOS (nix-darwin) configuration with Flakes and Home Manager.
 |---|---|---|---|---|
 | UM790 Pro | nixos | NixOS | x86_64-linux | ◎ |
 | M2 MacBook Air | darwin | macOS | aarch64-darwin | ◎ |
+| Galaxy S23 FE | Galaxy-S23FE | Android (nix-on-droid) | aarch64-linux | △ |
 
 
 ## Module Structure
 
 ```
-flake.nix                    # Entry point (nixosConfigurations + darwinConfigurations)
+flake.nix                    # Entry point (nixos + darwin + nixOnDroid Configurations)
 ├── nix/
 │   ├── hosts/
 │   │   ├── nixos/           # NixOS host config (boot, network, locale)
-│   │   └── darwin/          # macOS host config
+│   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS host config
+│   │   └── Galaxy-S23FE/    # nix-on-droid host config
 │   ├── profiles/
 │   │   ├── cli-minimal.nix  # Minimal CLI environment
 │   │   ├── cli.nix          # CLI environment (docker, tailscale)
@@ -30,6 +32,7 @@ flake.nix                    # Entry point (nixosConfigurations + darwinConfigur
 │   ├── modules/
 │   │   ├── linux/           # NixOS system modules (niri, docker, audio, etc.)
 │   │   ├── darwin/          # macOS nix-darwin modules (homebrew, system defaults)
+│   │   ├── nix-on-droid/    # nix-on-droid shared modules
 │   │   └── home/            # Home Manager shared modules (zsh, git, claude-code)
 │   └── overlays/            # Custom packages (overlay)
 ├── agents/skills/           # Claude Code agent skills
@@ -41,6 +44,7 @@ flake.nix                    # Entry point (nixosConfigurations + darwinConfigur

 - [docs/M2-MacBook-Air.md](docs/M2-MacBook-Air.md) - nix-darwin installation guide for macOS

--- a/nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix

-{ ... }:
+{ inputs }:
+let
+  inherit (inputs)
+    nixpkgs
+    nix-darwin
+    home-manager
+    nix-filter
+    moonbit-overlay
+    brew-nix
+    ;
 
-{
-  networking.hostName = "darwin";
+  system = "aarch64-darwin";
+
+  helpers = import ../../modules/lib/helpers { lib = nixpkgs.lib; };
+  dotfilesDir = "/Users/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix";
+  customOverlay = import ../../overlays;
+
+  local-skills = nix-filter.lib {
+    root = inputs.self;
+    include = [ "agents/skills" ];
+  };
+
+  externalOverlay = final: prev: {
+    claude-code = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.claude-code;
+    ccusage = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.ccusage;
+    codex = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.codex;
+    opencode = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.opencode;
+    vibe-kanban = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.vibe-kanban;
+    cursor-agent = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.cursor-agent;
+    gogcli = inputs.nix-steipete-tools.packages.${system}.gogcli;
+    version-lsp = inputs.version-lsp.packages.${system}.default.overrideAttrs (oldAttrs: {
+      doCheck = false;
+    });
+    gh-nippou = inputs.gh-nippou.packages.${system}.default;
+    gh-graph = inputs.gh-graph.packages.${system}.default;
+  };
+in
+nix-darwin.lib.darwinSystem {
+  inherit system;
+  specialArgs = {
+    inherit
+      helpers
+      dotfilesDir
+      home-manager
+      local-skills
+      ;
+    inherit (inputs)
+      anthropic-skills
+      vercel-skills
+      ui-ux-pro-max-skill
+      ast-grep-skill
+      agent-skills
+      onepassword-shell-plugins
+      ;
+  };
+  modules = [
+    ../../modules/darwin
+    ./configuration.nix
+    ../../profiles/darwin.nix
+    {
+      nixpkgs.overlays = [
+        externalOverlay
+        moonbit-overlay.overlays.default
+        customOverlay
+        brew-nix.overlays.default
+      ];
+      nixpkgs.config.allowUnfree = true;
+    }
+  ];
 }

> AGENT

修正内容:

- **HashiCorp**: `subdir` を `"skills"` → `"."` に修正（`terraform/`, `packer/` がルート直下のため）
- **enableAll**: `cloudflare`, `hashicorp`, `deno`, `aws` の4ソースを追加し、全スキルを有効化

これでビルド時に `specialArgs` → `agent-skills.nix` の引数 → `sources` → `enableAll` の流れが繋がります。

> AGENT

修正しました。スキルの受け渡しの流れは：

1. `flake.nix` inputs → `hosts/*/default.nix` の `specialArgs` → `modules/*/user.nix` の引数
2. `user.nix` の `extraSpecialArgs` → `modules/home/agent-skills.nix` の引数 → `sources` / `enableAll`

両方の `user.nix` で引数受け取りと `extraSpecialArgs` への追加が漏れていたので修正しました。

```json
{
  "repoName": "takeokunn/nixos-configuration",
  "question": "How is mcp-servers-nix configured? Show the flake input definition, how it's passed through to home-manager, and the actual MCP server configuration in home-manager modules."

> TOOL

tool_result
id: toolu_01Kqj7RE6CmQ8y8EeDXqnNQX
```
{"result":"The `mcp-servers-nix` flake input is defined in `flake.nix` . It is passed to Home Manager through `extraSpecialArgs` in the host configurations  . The actual MCP server configuration is then defined within the `claude-code` Home Manager program module, where `mcp-servers-nix.lib.evalModule` is used to enable and configure specific MCP servers .\n\n## Flake Input Definition\n\nThe `mcp-servers-nix` input is defined in `flake.nix` . It specifies the GitHub repository `natsukium/mcp-servers-nix` and follows the `nixpkgs` input for version consistency . The `flake.lock` file records its pinned version as `3b434a9aa4e7b119ff31d5dd726a28cac60b9d5d` from `natsukium/mcp-servers-nix` .\n\n```nix\n    mcp-servers-nix = {\n      url = \"github:natsukium/mcp-servers-nix\";\n      inputs.nixpkgs.follows = \"nixpkgs\";\n    };\n``` \n\n## Passing to Home Manager\n\nThe `mcp-servers-nix` input is passed to the Home Manager configuration via the `extraSpecialArgs` attribute in the host-specific `default.nix` files  .\n\nFor example, in the `X13Gen2` NixOS configuration, `mcp-servers-nix` is explicitly listed in `home-manager.extraSpecialArgs` :\n\n```nix\n      home-manager.extraSpecialArgs = {\n        inherit inputs system;\n        inherit (inputs)\n          nixpkgs\n          nixvim\n          mcp-servers-nix\n          llm-agents\n          anthropic-skills\n          cloudflare-skills\n          hashicorp-agent-skills\n          deno-skills\n          aws-agent-skills\n          microsoft-skills\n          nur-packages\n          emacs-overlay\n          org-babel\n          brew-nix\n          firefox-addons\n          ;\n      };\n``` \n\nSimilarly, for the `Attm-M4-Max` macOS configuration, `mcp-servers-nix` is passed through `extraSpecialArgs` to the Home Manager module :\n\n```nix\n        extraSpecialArgs = {\n          inherit inputs system username;\n          inherit (inputs) nixpkgs nixvim;\n          inherit (inputs) mcp-servers-nix;\n          inherit (inputs) llm-agents;\n          inherit (inputs) nur-packages;\n          inherit (inputs)\n            anthropic-skills\n            cloudflare-skills\n            hashicorp-agent-skills\n            deno-skills\n            aws-agent-skills\n            microsoft-skills\n            ;\n          inherit (inputs)\n            emacs-overlay\n            org-babel\n            brew-nix\n            firefox-addons\n            ;\n        };\n``` \n\nThe `home-manager/advanced.nix` module then receives `mcp-servers-nix` as an argument .\n\n## MCP Server Configuration in Home Manager\n\nWithin Home Manager, `mcp-servers-nix` is used in two primary ways:\n\n### 1. Overlay for Package Customization\n\nThe `mcp-servers-nix` input provides an overlay that is applied during the construction of the `pkgs` set in `home-manager/advanced.nix` . This overlay adds MCP server packages to the available package set .\n\n```nix\n    overlays =\n      basicOverlay ++ advancedOverlay ++ [ mcp-servers-nix.overlays.default ] ++ brewNixOverlay;\n``` \n\n### 2. `claude-code` Program Configuration\n\nThe actual configuration of MCP servers occurs within the `home-manager/programs/claude-code/default.nix` module . This module imports `mcp-servers-nix` directly .\n\nThe `claude-code` program uses `mcp-servers-nix.lib.evalModule` to define and enable specific MCP servers . For instance, `context7` is enabled, and `serena` is configured with `context = \"claude-code\"` and `enableWebDashboard = false` . Additionally, a `deepwiki` HTTP server is defined .\n\n```nix\n    mcpServers =\n      (mcp-servers-nix.lib.evalModule pkgs {\n        programs = {\n          context7.enable = true;\n          serena = {\n            enable = true;\n            context = \"claude-code\";\n            enableWebDashboard = false;\n          };\n        };\n      }).config.settings.servers\n      // {\n        deepwiki = {\n          type = \"http\";\n          url = \"https://mcp.deepwiki.com/mcp\";\n        };\n      };\n``` \n\nThe `claude-code` program also sets `enableAllProjectMcpServers = true` in its settings .\n\n## Notes\n\nThe `mcp-servers-nix` input is also mentioned in the `home-manager/overlay/advanced.nix` file , where it's used to override the `mcp` Python package to disable tests, which are flaky in the Nix sandbox . This is a separate usage from the direct server configuration.\n\nWiki pages you might want to explore:\n- [Flake System and Dependency Management (takeokunn/nixos-configuration)](/wiki/takeokunn/nixos-configuration#2)\n- [Home Manager Configuration (takeokunn/nixos-configuration)](/wiki/takeokunn/nixos-configuration#4)\n\nView this search on DeepWiki: https://deepwiki.REDACTED\n"}
```

Perfect! Now I have comprehensive information. Let me create a final summary document with all the findings organized by the three questions asked:

## Investigation Complete: mcp-servers-nix Integration Guide

Based on my thorough investigation of mcp-servers-nix, your dotnix repository structure, and reference implementations, here's the complete breakdown:

---

## 1. MCP Servers Available in mcp-servers-nix & Configuration API

### Available Servers (25+ modules)

**Core Utilities:**
- `filesystem` - filesystem access with path restrictions
- `git` - Git repository operations
- `fetch` - HTTP fetch operations
- `time` - Time/date utilities
- `memory` - Memory management

**Cloud & Integration:**
- `github` - GitHub API integration
- `notion` - Notion database access
- `grafana` - Grafana metrics
- `tavily` - Web search
- `deepl` - Translation service

**Development Tools:**
- `terraform` - Terraform operations
- `nixos` - NixOS configuration operations
- `playwright` - Browser automation
- `sequential-thinking` - Reasoning
- `textlint` - Text linting

**Other Services:**
- `clickup`, `esa`, `slite`, `mastra`, `context7`, `everything`, `netdata`, `codex`, `serena`

### Configuration API: `mkConfig`

**Function Signature:**
```nix
mcp-servers-nix.lib.mkConfig pkgs {
  programs = { ... };
  format = "json";        # or "yaml"
  flavor = "claude";      # or "vscode", "codex", "zed"
  fileName = "...";
  settings = { ... };     # custom merging
}
```

**Program Configuration Pattern:**
```nix
programs = {
  filesystem = {
    enable = true;
    args = [ "/path/to/allowed/directory" ];
  };
  git.enable = true;
  github = {
    enable = true;
    envFile = "/path/to/.env";  # or passwordCommand
  };
}
```

**Output Flavors & Key Names:**

| Flavor | Key Name | File | Client |
|--------|----------|------|--------|
| `claude` | `mcpServers` | `claude_desktop_config.json` | Claude Desktop |
| `vscode` | `mcp.servers` | `settings.json` | VS Code |
| `codex` | `mcp_servers` | `.mcp.toml` | Codex CLI |
| `zed` | `context_servers` | varies | Zed |

**Security Notes:**
- Use `envFile = "/path/to/.env"` for credential files
- Or `passwordCommand = { VAR_NAME = [ "pass" "show" "path" ]; }`
- Never hardcode secrets in `env` (they'd be exposed in /nix/store)

---

## 2. Your dotnix Repository Structure & Input Flow

### Architecture Pattern

Your configuration follows this input flow:

```
flake.nix (inputs definition)
  ↓
  ├→ nix/hosts/{nixos,darwin}/default.nix
  │   (extracts inputs, creates specialArgs)
  │   ↓
  │   └→ nix/modules/{linux,darwin}/user.nix
  │       (passes specialArgs via extraSpecialArgs)
  │       ↓
  │       └→ nix/modules/home/default.nix
  │           (imports all home modules including claude-code.nix)
  │
  └→ specialArgs & extraSpecialArgs receive:
     - helpers, dotfilesDir, niri
     - agent-skills, anthropic-skills, vercel-skills, etc.
```

### Current Input Declarations (flake.nix, lines 4-107)

Your flake already has the pattern for adding inputs:

```nix
inputs = {
  nixpkgs.url = "github:NixOS/nixpkgs/nixos-unstable";
  home-manager = { ... };
  agent-skills.url = "github:Kyure-A/agent-skills-nix";
  # ... other inputs
};
```

### How Inputs Flow to Home Modules

**In nix/hosts/nixos/default.nix (lines 40-59):**
```nix
specialArgs = {
  inherit
    helpers
    dotfilesDir
    home-manager
    niri
    local-skills
    ;
  inherit (inputs)
    anthropic-skills
    vercel-skills
    agent-skills
    # ... skills passed here
    ;
};
```

**In nix/modules/linux/user.nix (lines 26-40):**
```nix
home-manager = {
  extraSpecialArgs = {
    inherit
      helpers
      dotfilesDir
      niri
      local-skills
      anthropic-skills
      # ... passed to home modules
      ;
  };
```

**In home modules like agent-skills.nix:**
```nix
{
  pkgs,
  local-skills,
  anthropic-skills,
  # ... received as parameters
  ...
}:
```

---

## 3. Current claude-code.nix Configuration

### Location
`/home/yuta/ghq/github.com/yutakobayashidev/dotnix/nix/modules/home/programs/claude-code.nix`

### Current Structure (lines 20-58)

```nix

  "$schema" = "https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json";
  env = { ... };
  enabledPlugins = { ... };
  language = "Japanese";
  skipDangerousModePermissionPrompt = true;
  statusline = { ... };

};
```

### **No mcpServers Section Currently Exists**

The current configuration generates settings.json but does NOT have an `mcpServers` attribute. This is where you'd add MCP server configuration.

---

## 4. Integration Pattern for mcp-servers-nix

### Two Recommended Approaches

#### **Approach A: Using mkConfig Directly (More Control)**

Add to flake.nix inputs:
```nix
mcp-servers-nix.url = "github:natsukium/mcp-servers-nix";
```

Then in your claude-code.nix module:
```nix
{
  pkgs,
  mcp-servers-nix,
  dotfilesDir,
  ...
}:

let
  mcpConfig = mcp-servers-nix.lib.mkConfig pkgs {
    programs = {
      filesystem = {
        enable = true;
        args = [ "${config.home.homeDirectory}/Projects" ];
      };
      git.enable = true;
      github = {
        enable = true;
        envFile = "${dotfilesDir}/secrets/github.env";
      };
    };
    flavor = "claude";
    fileName = "claude_desktop_config.json";
  };
in
{
  xdg.configFile."claude/settings.json".text = builtins.toJSON (
    (builtins.fromJSON mcpConfig).mcpServers
    # ... merge with existing settings
  );
}
```

#### **Approach B: Using Home-Manager Module (If Available)**

Similar to how you handle agent-skills with `home-manager.sharedModules`:
```nix
home-manager = {
  sharedModules = [ 
    agent-skills.homeManagerModules.default
    # mcp-servers-nix.homeManagerModules.default (if available)
  ];
}
```

#### **Approach C: Manual JSON Construction (Simple)**

Directly add mcpServers to your settings.json:
```nix
xdg.configFile."claude/settings.json".text = builtins.toJSON {
  "$schema" = "https://json.schemastore.org/claude-code-settings.json";
  # ... existing config
  
  mcpServers = {
    filesystem = {
      command = "${pkgs.mcp-servers.filesystem}/bin/mcp-server-filesystem";
      args = [ "${config.home.homeDirectory}/Projects" ];
    };
    git = {
      command = "${pkgs.mcp-servers.git}/bin/mcp-server-git";
    };
  };
};
```

---

## 5. Reference Implementations

### mcps.nix Pattern
Uses two home-manager modules:
- `inputs.mcps.homeManagerModules.claude` - stores servers in Nix store
- `inputs.mcps.homeManagerModules.claude-install` - stores in home directory

```nix
programs.claude-code = {
  enable = true;
  mcps = {
    git.enable = true;
    filesystem = {
      enable = true;
      allowedPaths = [ "${config.home.homeDirectory}/Projects" ];
    };
  };
}
```

### Key Pattern You Already Use
Your agent-skills configuration is the perfect template:

**agent-skills.nix structure:**
1. Function takes `pkgs` + input parameters
2. Enables programs with configuration
3. Sets targets for deployment locations
4. Uses home-manager's `programs.*` namespace

**You can apply the same pattern for mcp-servers:**
1. Pass `mcp-servers-nix` as an input (like you do with `agent-skills`)
2. Import it in specialArgs flow
3. Create an mcp-servers.nix home module
4. Generate mcpServers JSON in claude-code.nix

---

## 6. Recommended Integration Steps

Given your architecture, here's the cleanest approach:

### Step 1: Add to flake.nix
```nix
inputs = {
  # ... existing inputs
  mcp-servers-nix.url = "github:natsukium/mcp-servers-nix";
}
```

### Step 2: Pass to specialArgs (both nixos and darwin)
In `nix/hosts/{nixos,darwin}/default.nix`, add to `specialArgs`:
```nix
inherit (inputs) mcp-servers-nix;
```

### Step 3: Pass to home-manager extraSpecialArgs
In `nix/modules/{linux,darwin}/user.nix`:
```nix
extraSpecialArgs = {
  inherit mcp-servers-nix;
  # ... existing
}
```

### Step 4: Create mcp-servers.nix home module
New file: `nix/modules/home/mcp-servers.nix`
```nix
{ pkgs, mcp-servers-nix, dotfilesDir, ... }:
let
  mcpConfig = mcp-servers-nix.lib.mkConfig pkgs {
    programs = {
      filesystem.enable = true;
      git.enable = true;
      github = {
        enable = true;
        envFile = "${dotfilesDir}/secrets/github.env";
      };
    };
    flavor = "claude";
    fileName = "claude_desktop_config.json";
  };
in
{
  # Export for use in claude-code.nix
  home.activation.mcpServers = { text = ""; };
  _mcp = builtins.fromJSON mcpConfig;
}
```

### Step 5: Integrate into claude-code.nix
Merge mcpServers into the settings.json generation.

---

## Key Takeaways

1. **mkConfig is the primary API** - generates JSON for different clients automatically
2. **Your input flow pattern already supports this** - just add mcp-servers-nix like agent-skills
3. **Two places for server configuration:**
   - Program definitions in mkConfig (`programs.filesystem = {...}`)
   - Output flavor determines JSON key name (`mcpServers` for Claude Desktop)
4. **Credentials are secured** via `envFile` or `passwordCommand`, not hardcoded
5. **Modular** - combine multiple servers declaratively
6. **25+ pre-built servers** ready to use without custom compilation

- [MCP Servers Nix on LobeHub](https://lobehub.com/mcp/natsukium-mcp-servers-nix)

## Approved Plan:
# mcp-servers-nix 統合計画

## Context

現在 `claude-code.nix` で `settings.json` を生成しているが、`mcpServers` の設定がない。takeokunn/nixos-configuration を参考に、`mcp-servers-nix` を使って MCP サーバーを宣言的に管理する。

## 変更ファイル

### 1. `flake.nix` — input 追加

```nix
mcp-servers-nix = {
  url = "github:natsukium/mcp-servers-nix";
  inputs.nixpkgs.follows = "nixpkgs";
};
```

### 2. `nix/hosts/nixos/default.nix` / `nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix` — specialArgs に追加

```nix
inherit (inputs) mcp-servers-nix;
```

### 3. `nix/modules/linux/user.nix` / `nix/modules/darwin/user.nix` — 引数 + extraSpecialArgs に追加

```nix
mcp-servers-nix,  # 引数
# ...
extraSpecialArgs = {
  inherit mcp-servers-nix;
  # ...
};
```

### 4. `nix/modules/home/programs/claude-code.nix` — mcpServers 生成

`mcp-servers-nix.lib.evalModule` で MCP サーバー設定を生成し、`settings.json` の `mcpServers` に追加。

```nix
{ pkgs, mcp-servers-nix, ... }:
let
  mcpServers =
    (mcp-servers-nix.lib.evalModule pkgs {
      programs = {
        context7.enable = true;
      };
    }).config.settings.servers
    // {
      deepwiki = {
        type = "http";
        url = "https://mcp.deepwiki.com/mcp";
      };
    };
in
{
  xdg.configFile."claude/settings.json".text = builtins.toJSON {
    # ... 既存設定
    inherit mcpServers;
  };
}
```

## 受け渡しフロー

```
flake.nix inputs
  → hosts/*/default.nix specialArgs
    → modules/*/user.nix extraSpecialArgs
      → modules/home/programs/claude-code.nix
```

## 検証

```bash
nix run .#build
```

> AGENT

`niri` は `specialArgs` 経由ではなくモジュールとして使われているだけです。`specialArgs` で `niri` を渡しているのは `user.nix` の `extraSpecialArgs` のためだけ。`helpers` と `dotfilesDir` も同様。つまり `specialArgs` は完全に不要にできます。

では実装します。

nix-filter.lib {\n    root = inputs.self;\n    include = [ \"agents/skills\" ];\n  };\n\n  externalOverlay = final: prev: {\n    claude-code = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.claude-code;\n    ccusage = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.ccusage;\n    codex = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.codex;\n    opencode = inputs.llm-agents.packages.${system}.opencode;\n    vibe-kanban = inputs.gh-graph.packages.${system}.default;\n    ghostty = inputs.ghostty.packages.${system}.default;\n  };\nin\nnixpkgs.lib.nixosSystem {\n  inherit system;\n  modules = [\n inputs.nixpkgs.legacyPackages.${system}.zsh;\n        extraGroups = [\n          \"networkmanager\"\n          \"wheel\"\n          \"docker\"\n          \"adbusers\"\n        ];\n      };\n\n      nix.settings.allowed-users = [ \"yuta\" ];\n      nix.settings.trusted-users = [\n        \"root\"\n        \"yuta\"\n      ];\n    }\n  ];\n}\n"

> AGENT

ビルド成功です。変更の要約：

- **`nix/modules/linux/user.nix`** / **`nix/modules/darwin/user.nix`**: 削除
- **`nix/modules/linux/default.nix`** / **`nix/modules/darwin/default.nix`**: `user.nix` の import を削除
- **`nix/hosts/nixos/default.nix`**: home-manager設定・ユーザー設定をインラインモジュールとして追加。`specialArgs` は `niri` のみ（`gui.nix` が参照するため）
- **`nix/hosts/darwin/default.nix`**: 同様にhome-manager設定をインラインモジュールとして追加。`specialArgs` は不要に

これで `specialArgs` → `user.nix` → `extraSpecialArgs` のバケツリレーが解消され、スキルやMCPの追加時に `user.nix` を触る必要がなくなりました。

> AGENT

`gui.nix` が NixOS モジュール引数として `niri` を受け取り、16行目で `niri.homeModules.niri` を home-manager に渡しています。NixOS モジュールの引数は `specialArgs` 経由でしか渡せないので、`niri` だけ `specialArgs` に残す必要がありました。

他のスキル系（`anthropic-skills` 等）は home-manager の `extraSpecialArgs` だけで済むので `specialArgs` は不要です。

ただこの `gui.nix` の設計自体、`niri` flake input を `specialArgs` で引き回すのは微妙です。ホストの `default.nix` に `niri.homeModules.niri` の import を移せば `specialArgs` を完全に消せます。やりますか？

> AGENT

moons-14/dotfiles のパターンが分かりました。

**moons-14 のアプローチ**: `specialArgs = { inherit inputs; }` で `inputs` を丸ごと渡し、各モジュールで `{ inputs, ... }:` として受け取る。niri の home module では `imports = [ inputs.niri-flake.homeModules.niri ];` のように `inputs` 経由で直接参照。

**現状のあなたの構成**: `niri` を個別に `specialArgs` で渡し、`gui.nix` で `{ niri, ... }:` として受け取っている。

つまり `specialArgs = { inherit inputs; }` にすれば、`gui.nix` で `{ inputs, ... }:` として `inputs.niri.homeModules.niri` を参照でき、個別の `specialArgs` が完全に不要になります。takeokunn のリポジトリも同じく `inputs` 丸ごと渡しのパターンです。

> AGENT

ビルド成功です。変更の要約:

- **`gui.nix`**: `{ niri, ... }` → `{ inputs, ... }` に変更、`inputs.niri.homeModules.niri` で参照
- **`hosts/nixos/default.nix`** / **`hosts/darwin/default.nix`**: `specialArgs = { inherit inputs; }` に統一。`extraSpecialArgs` も `inputs` 丸ごと渡しに変更し、個別スキルの列挙を廃止
- **`agent-skills.nix`**: 個別引数 → `inputs` 経由 (`inputs.anthropic-skills` 等) に変更
- **`claude-code.nix`**: `mcp-servers-nix` → `inputs.mcp-servers-nix` に変更
- **`modules/linux/user.nix`** / **`modules/darwin/user.nix`**: 削除
- **`modules/{linux,darwin}/default.nix`**: `user.nix` の import を削除

これで新しい flake input を追加する際、hosts の `specialArgs` や `extraSpecialArgs` を触る必要がなくなりました。home モジュールで `inputs.xxx` として直接参照できます。

> DEVELOPER

inputs.niri.homeModules.niriなんてmoonsのにないと思うdna毛度、なんで？

> AGENT

moons-14 は `imports = [ inputs.niri-flake.homeModules.niri ]` を **home-manager モジュール** (`modules/home/niri/default.nix`) の中に書いています。

うちの `gui.nix` は NixOS プロファイル（システムモジュール）なのに、そこに `home-manager.users.yuta.imports` で niri の homeModule を入れている。本来は home-manager 側のモジュール（`nix/modules/linux/programs/niri.nix`）に書くべきです。そうすれば `gui.nix` は `inputs` を参照する必要がなくなります。

> AGENT

`niri.nix` は home-manager モジュールなので `extraSpecialArgs` 経由で `inputs` を受け取ります。システムモジュール側では `inputs` を使っていないので、`specialArgs` から `inputs` を外せます。ただし、将来の拡張性を考えると `specialArgs = { inherit inputs; }` を残しておくのも合理的です。コストゼロなので残しておきます。

> DEVELOPER

inheritで元々やっていたのはなぜ？

> AGENT

`inherit` で個別に渡していた理由は、**明示的な依存関係の宣言**というNixの設計哲学に沿ったものです。

各モジュールが `{ anthropic-skills, niri, ... }:` のように必要な入力だけを引数に取ることで：

1. **そのモジュールが何に依存しているか一目でわかる** — 関数シグネチャが依存関係のドキュメントになる
2. **不要な入力へのアクセスを防げる** — モジュールが知る必要のないものに触れない
3. **渡し忘れるとビルドエラーで即座にわかる** — 暗黙の依存が生まれない

ただし、その代償が「バケツリレー」問題です。新しいinputを追加するたびに `flake.nix → hosts → user.nix → extraSpecialArgs → モジュール` の全段を更新する必要がありました。今回のcloudflare/hashicorp/deno/awsスキル追加で、その手間が顕著になったわけです。

`inputs` を丸ごと渡すパターン（moons-14やtakeokunn方式）は逆のトレードオフで、**利便性と引き換えに依存関係が暗黙的になる**。ただ、dotfilesのような個人設定リポジトリでは、厳密な依存管理よりメンテナンス性の方が重要なので、こちらの方が実用的です。
